公司簡介 
亞德客是一家專業生產各類氣動器材的集
團 ， 主要從事生產氣動控制元件 、 氣動執行
原件 、 氣源處理元件及氣動輔助元件之製造
及銷售等業務，以 「AirTAC」 自有品牌銷售
於大中華地區，中國市場佔營收 94％，台
灣佔 2％、海外佔 4％，現為中國市佔排名
第二的氣動元件廠，市佔率為 30％，SMC
在中國市佔率排名第一，達 33％。

主要競爭對手：SMC、Festo

林其美 iris.lin@sinopac.com 
本次報告：座談會

亞德客-KY (1590 TT) 
成長勢頭延續至 2027 年

永豐觀點 
2Q26 進入旺季、營運將來到新高，全年營收成長率調升至 20%以上，2027 年預
計仍在成長軌道上。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進建議：理由包括：(1)2Q26 進入旺季、營運將來到新高，全年營收成長率
調升至 20%以上，季度間淡旺季回到常軌，2027 年預計仍在成長軌道上，(2) 5 月
中國政府出台新社保提撥方案，且追溯至 1 月，因此五月與六月將補提追溯部分，
預計營業利益率將減少 2%，全年營業利益率維持 33%目標，(3) 2026 年線軌市場
將回溫，預計年底時稼動率可達五成，2027 年營業利益率近氣動元件。評價方面，
公司本益比區間 20-40 倍，行業景氣回溫伴隨公司營運穩健成長，2026 年獲利持
續成長 ， 且2027 年成長勢頭延續 ， 維持買進建議 ， 目標價1972 元（29X 2027EPS）。

ESG 評析 
亞德客企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向
平均得分分別為 A 等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。
SinoPac Securities Investment Service licenses and applies the SASB Materiality Map® 
General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

### Page 2

2

亞德客為中國第二大氣動元件廠商：亞德客成立於 1989 年，2009 年設立於開曼
群島的亞德客國際為控股主體 ， 是一家專業生產各類氣動器材的集團， 公司主要從
事生產氣動控制元件、 氣動執行原件、 氣源處理元件及氣動輔助元件之製造及銷售
等業務，以「AirTAC」自有品牌銷售於大中華地區，中國市場佔營收 94％，台灣
佔 2％、海外佔4％，現為中國市佔排名第二的氣動元件廠，市佔率為30％，SMC
在中國市佔率排名第一，達 33％。 
2026 年全年營收將達 20%以上 （再度調高） ，營業利益率33%以上：今年以來前
5 月出貨量比公司預期好，5 月每日出貨量僅低於四月一些，不過因假日較多，營
收乃低於四月，至於 6 月每日出貨金額與五月差不多，總之 2Q26 營收將會來到
新高水準。行業應用別當中，電子業占公司營收 26%，今年成長幅度達 20%，這
當中來自於手機僅成長個位數，主要乃是因 AI 興起所帶動的生產線更換進而推升
氣動元件的需求， 公司營收當中約有六成來自於客戶擴廠所帶動的需求， 四成來自
於既有產線更換氣動元件需求（氣動元件屬耗材，1-3 年更換一次） ；汽車行業的
營收成長主要來自於公司市佔率的提升，電池行業今年成長幅度預計可達 50%-
60%，傳統行業的氣動元件需求穩健成長與自動化程度提升有關。整體而言，公司
預計全年營收可成長 20%以上 （上一次預估可成長 15%-20%） 。 至於營業利益率 ，
4 月營業利益率達 36%，來到新高水準，不過5 月中國政府出台新社保提撥方案，
且追溯至 1 月，因此5 月與 6 月將補提追溯部分，預計營業利益率將減少 2%，不
過 2Q26 營業利益率仍將優於 1Q26。 
線軌市場逐漸回溫 ， 預計年底時稼動率可達五成 ，2027 年營業利益率近氣動元件 ：
線軌市場景氣約落後氣動元件行業 2-3 季度，氣動元件行業已處於復甦狀態，預
料線軌行業 2026 年有機會回溫。公司線軌2025 年出貨量成長 20%，產能利用率
約 20%-30%，隨著需求回溫，產能利用率已達40%，預計到年底時可再進一步提
升至 50%，屆時毛利率可達 30%，營業利益率與氣動元件的差距將逐漸縮小。公
司預計 2027 年線軌的營業利益率將接近氣動的營業利益率 ， 若產能利用率再提升
至 90%-100%，公司依現有機台啟動三班制生產（目前兩班制） 。公司預計未來十
年線軌營收可達到 30 億元人民幣。 
資本支出維持，股利配發率提升：公司今年資本支出預估 20-30 億元，2027 年也
將維持在這樣的水準，2028 年因應電控產品量產的需求，資本支出可能會高於30
億元。現階段資本支出的水準可支應公司每年 20%銷售量的成長，公司可透過三
班制或加班方式提升產量。 
財務預估： 預估2026 年合併營收為 428.06 億元(+24.69％YoY)， 稅後淨利114.47
億元(+36.28％YoY)，EPS 為 57.24 元。預估 2027 年合併營收為 507.24 億元
(+18.5％YoY)，稅後淨利 136 億元(+18.8％YoY)，EPS 為 68 元。

營運現況與分析

### Page 1

20260612  艦長紀要
ComputeX 2026 會後紀要 ＋ Q&A 重點 ・ 圖文整合版
主題：AI 算力供應鏈、Token 經濟學、CPO 光通訊與先進封裝
來源：永豐金證券 Institutional Business Group / J&J Investment 大會
日期：2026 年 6 月 12 日　｜　對象：基金經理人與產業分析師
本版說明
本紀要整合兩段現場錄音之文字摘要（① 大會紀要 4 頁、② Q&A 重點 3 頁）與 18 張投影片，重繪清晰中文圖表並新
增技術解說示意圖，逐一說明 NVL76 跨機櫃、XO 外部光引擎、大立光 Fiber Array、SoIC 3D 堆疊、800V 供電等關
鍵概念，供經理人與分析師快速吸收。
※ 文中圖表多為依紀要與投影片數據重繪之示意圖，個別點位為近似值，請以原始資料為準。
本文件僅供內部投資研究與教育訓練使用，不構成投資建議或要約；市場預估具不確定性，買賣請自行判斷。

### Page 2

20260612 艦長紀要 ・ 完整整合版 ComputeX 2026 會後 ＋ Q&A 重點
核心結論｜艦長一頁看懂
一句話：AI  需求與變現（Token  經濟學）正進入營收爆發期，先進封裝與光通訊（CPO/XO）是下世代主戰
場、且台積電一條龍掌握；但 SOX 評價已回到約 30 倍本益比的循環高點，下游庫存偏高，須緊盯獲利兌現
速度。
• 基本面續上修：大會調研估 2026 全年營收成長動能約 60%、2027 年初估 >45%；2 月修正被證實為良性
漲多回檔。
• 庫存體檢：晶圓代工端健康；下游伺服器/網通/ODM 庫存偏高但正實質去化；PC 因 DDR5 被動拉貨備貨
；車用與代理商庫存偏低。
• Token 經濟學爆發：Anthropic ARR 一年半成長約 40 倍至 400–500 億美元，年底目標 1,000–1,200 億；
與 OpenAI 合計今年跨越 1,500 億美元。
• 差別定價：低價值 Token 容忍延遲、付費企業每百萬 Token 收 40–50 美元 → 營收優於預期，刺激 CSP

再上修 2027 資本支出（市場共識 8,000–9,000 億美元）。
• CPO/XO 是關鍵轉折：448G 以上銅線 PCB 僅能傳 5 公分，全面倒向光通訊；XO 外部光引擎頻寬達 12.8
T；台積電深度綁定 Ayar Labs。
• NVL76 架構：以 CPO 跨 8 機櫃互聯（9+18+9 配置、Quantum-5 交換晶片）；單一 GPU 直接對應 6 個
3.2T 光模組 → 光通訊需求 6 倍爆發。
• 大立光 Fiber Array 突破：交付 4 排 160 條高密度光纖陣列、V-Groove 公差僅 0.3µm，成台積電 1.6T/3.
2T 封裝關鍵夥伴。
• 先進封裝核心爭點：客戶『離不開台積電』；Intel  E-interconnect  卡在  Mix-Bump  良率；若  ASIC  受阻
，最大利多是 NVIDIA。
• 處理器趨勢：ARM 在超大型資料中心市佔數年內將過半超越 x86；四大 CSP 自研 CPU（Graviton/Axion
/Cobalt/Meta）齊發；800V 高壓供電提早導入。

操作定調
題材方向明確向上：續抱『先進封裝（CoWoS→SoIC）＋  光通訊/CPO/XO  ＋  ASIC  設計服務  ＋  電源/散熱』四大主
線；但 SOX 約 30x PE、PB 創高，指數型部位控管乖離、緊盯庫存去化與獲利兌現，題材股逢回布局優於追高。
僅供內部投資研究參考・嚴禁外流 1

### Page 3

20260612 艦長紀要 ・ 完整整合版 ComputeX 2026 會後 ＋ Q&A 重點
第一部・大會紀要
一、總體基本面與供應鏈庫存體檢
大會調研顯示全球半導體與 AI 產業基本面持續上修，市場強勁動能未改；2 月的劇烈修正被證實僅為良性的
漲多回檔，產業健康度無虞。
• 產業營收動能：預估  2026  全年營收成長動能達  60%，外圍市場與主要板塊對  2027  年初估亦達  45%  以
上。
• 上游庫存：晶圓代工端庫存處於相對正常的健康水平、無過剩風險，生產天數控制在合理區間。
• 下游與網通：伺服器大廠（Supermicro、Dell、HPE）、ODM/OEM（鴻海、廣達、仁寶）及網通（Cisc
o、Arista、Celestica）庫存處相對高點，正實質去化，天數略高仍可控。
• 消費性：PC  的  DDR5  庫存出現明顯被動拉貨與備貨潮（下半年季節性需求帶動）；車用測試與代理商端
庫存普遍偏低。
• 評價面：4、5 月股價飆升，SOX 本益比回到約 30 倍歷史高位、PB 同步創高，須密切追蹤後續獲利實質

兌現速度。
圖1：SOX 與台灣14家IC設計庫存趨勢（藍柱金額、橙線天數、紅虛線三年均值）。金額創高，但天數約120–125天貼近均值。
僅供內部投資研究參考・嚴禁外流 2

圖2：SOX 本益比（約30x）與股價淨值比（約11x）皆處循環高點，評價偏高。

### Page 5

20260612 艦長紀要 ・ 完整整合版 ComputeX 2026 會後 ＋ Q&A 重點
二、AI 市場需求與 Token 經濟學
大模型廠（LLM）的算力消耗與  Token  使用量正在重塑整個軟硬體產業鏈的利潤分配，Token  經濟學已迎
來實質營收爆發期。
• 算力與用量反彈：今年 Token 總使用量階梯式走高；4 月因模型廠調價、部分廠商算力不足（Anthropic
算力『擠兌』、限制客戶使用天數）短期用量下滑，隨基礎建設補強（與 xAI 等敲定大型資料中心協議）
，瓶頸打開、用量大幅反彈。
• ARR 幾何級數增長：Anthropic ARR 由去年 1 月的 1–2 億美元飆升至 400–500 億美元，一年半約 40 倍
，年底目標 1,000–1,200 億；與 OpenAI 合計今年跨越 1,500 億美元。
• 資本支出上修：現金快速回籠刺激下，各大 CSP 預計再上修 2027 年資本支出（2026 預估已達 7,000 億
美元，2027 市場共識 8,000–9,000 億美元）。

圖3：模型廠『差別定價』策略——低價值 Token 容忍延遲、付費企業每百萬 Token 收 40–50 美元。
圖4：Anthropic ARR 一年半成長約 40 倍（示意）。

三、共同光學封裝（CPO/XO）與台積電下世代架構
傳統銅線在高頻已達物理極限：速度推升至 448G 以上時訊號損耗極快，傳統 PCB 板上傳輸距離壓縮至僅剩
5 公分，推動下世代架構全面倒向光通訊。
圖6：448G 以上銅線 PCB 傳輸僅剩 5 公分，全面轉向光通訊。
台積電與 Ayar Labs 深層綁定：在 CPO 領域，由 Intel、NVIDIA、台積電共同投資的新創 Ayar Labs（星路
科技）扮演核心角色；台積電投資後已將其技術與自身先進製程及封裝平台深度整合（原仰賴 GlobalFound
ries 代工，現全面轉向台積電製程）。
圖7：XO 外部光引擎——尺寸約傳統兩倍、不放 DSP，以 64 通道×200G 將頻寬推升至 12.8T，省電且改善過熱。

### Page 10

20260612 艦長紀要 ・ 完整整合版 ComputeX 2026 會後 ＋ Q&A 重點
四、晶圓代工先進製程與處理器競爭
圖10：先進封裝由 2.5D CoWoS 走向 3D SoIC；HBM 控制層與核心 IO 優先採垂直整合。
• 台積電 SoIC（3D 堆疊）：晶片物理尺寸逼近 Reticle 限制、良率成本拉高，封裝策略由 2.5D CoWoS 全
面移向 3D SoIC；HBM 記憶體控制層及核心 IO 元件未來優先以 3D 垂直整合。
• NVIDIA  Vera  處理器：核心策略『買處理器直接綁定  HBM  產能』，在先進封裝與高階記憶體極度短缺下
精準解決客戶產能痛點，年底前可望挹注龐大營收。
• Google TPU v6（Octopus）：明年（2027）市場上唯一採超大尺寸（達 8–9 個 Reticle 以上）的超巨型
先進封裝晶片，內含 4 個運算 die、4 個 IO 控制 die 及 8 顆高階 HBM；其製程良率與出貨進度將成為全
球先進代工產能與良率的最高指標。

圖11：AI 晶片整合面積/光罩倍數逐年放大（1 reticle=858mm²）。

五、AI 架構與晶片外掛光學（CPO/OEO）時程
• 800G 與超大型 Scale-Out 網路：業內高度關注的 NVL76 與下一代超大集群架構，明（2027）年搭載 NV
IDIA 新平台量產趨勢明確，後（2028）年起大幅擴展。
• CPO/OEO 切換節點：市場傳言 CPO 可能於 2029 年導入。艦長指出若 2029 年將 OEO 封裝直接整合或
大舉導入 GPU 旁，確實符合全光網路進程；惟短中期（約 2028 年）在 48V Ready 電力架構下，高速訊
號無法再走 PCB，訊號從處理器拉出後將全面走向光通訊或專屬高速連接結構。
• Spectrum-6 交換機：Broadcom 主導的 Spectrum-6 方案預計於明（2027）年起在大型雲端客戶（如 G
oogle）進行數萬台規模的初期量產與測試。
六、大客戶 ASIC 與主要 Foundry／Service 供應商競爭

【觀察】Google 意圖提升聯發科供貨比重以降低對博通、美滿的依賴；艦長評論，將 ASIC 代工 100% 移轉
屬極劇烈的供應鏈結構轉變，背後常涉及非技術性的特定商業考量，而非單純分散風險。

### Page 13

20260612 艦長紀要 ・ 完整整合版 ComputeX 2026 會後 ＋ Q&A 重點
七、先進封裝良率瓶頸與技術變革（E-interconnect vs CoWoS）
【核心投資爭點】客戶會因封裝瓶頸離開台積電嗎？
艦長給予明確強烈指引：絕對沒有任何主要客戶能真正離開台積電。Google、博通等雲端/晶片大廠並非傳統半導體
大廠，對先進製程與封裝缺乏長期底層信賴與自主研發根基；非台積電陣營（Intel、Samsung）在先進製程與封裝
『踩雷』機率極高，大客戶屢經挫折後仍會回歸台積電。博通曾計畫將晶片全數轉往  Intel  代工，最終受限技術成熟
度仍須依賴台積電。
• 翹曲（Warping）問題：大客戶下一代晶片  Die  Size  過大，傳統  CoWoS  出現翹曲；解方包括將  Carrier
換為玻璃基板（Glass Substrate）以提升平整度、降低翹曲。
• Intel  E-interconnect：技術概念先進，但最大難點在  Mix-Bump（混合凸塊）製程——主晶片需融合兩

種不同  Foundry  製程，必須同時處理  Micro-Bump  與  Standard-Bump，應力與焊接良率挑戰極大，目
前仍處研發與小規模測試。
• 對 NVIDIA 之利弊：若大客戶晶片因封裝良率無法突破而出貨延遲，對 NVIDIA 是最大利多——NVIDIA 已
在  CoWoS  產能、先進散熱、HBM  等周邊與一線供應商提早簽訂排他/優先供應，美系雲端自研  ASIC  受
阻時，算力需求將全數倒向 NVIDIA。
八、CPU 市場與 CSP 自主架構趨勢
• ARM 全面超越 x86：艦長預估超大型資料中心中 ARM 架構 CPU 市佔數年內將正式超越 50%；Intel 雖開
始為 CSP 代工伺服器處理器，但進度落後、難逆轉。
• 第三方 ARM 處理器：NVIDIA Grace、博通、高通積極搶進；ARM 官方自推微架構受商業模式限制，資料
中心 CPU 直接市佔反不及上述大廠。
四大 CSP 自研 CPU 狀態
Amazon Graviton 系列 成熟度最高

Google Axion 全面推動自研
Microsoft Cobalt 已推出自研
Meta 與 SocialNexus 合作開發 預計今年（2026）起陸續開出規模
僅供內部投資研究參考・嚴禁外流 12

九、電力與超高壓供電架構（800V）
圖13：機櫃功耗由 120kW 升至 200kW+，導入 800V 高壓供電與 HD-VPC（高密度垂直供電）。
• 800V  的必然性：AI  機櫃功耗由  120kW  升至下一代  200kW+，低壓高電流模式線路損耗（I²R）嚴重，直
接導入 800V 高壓＋HD-VPC 已成必經之路。
• 導入時程提前：原預期下一代更遠架構才普及，但調研顯示大客戶與  NVIDIA  在最新先進機櫃（B200/Ult
ra、後續 Rubin 周邊）已提早將 800V（含 54V 轉高壓等方案）列為積極導入項目，加速 PMIC、高壓 M
OSFET、變壓元件供應鏈產值提升。
2026–2029 關鍵晶片及技術進程預估表

Google TPU B9 Marvell / 博通 量產中 傳 Google 擬提高聯發科比重以分散風險

Broadcom Sunfish Ultra（W
halefish）
博通 2027–2028 雙晶片封裝，主打 CPO 與光通訊 Rack 整合
Broadcom Mirage（TPU-lik
e）

### Page 15

20260612 艦長紀要 ・ 完整整合版 ComputeX 2026 會後 ＋ Q&A 重點
第三部・投資結論與受惠地圖
四大結構性主線（逢回布局、核心持有）
• 先進封裝（CoWoS→SoIC）：整合面積指數放大、良率成本推升  3D  堆疊需求；台積電一條龍，封測（
日月光、京元電）、探針卡（FormFactor、旺矽  MPI）、玻璃基板、測試機台（愛德萬、Teradyne、C
hroma）受惠。
• 光通訊／CPO／XO：448G 以上全面轉光，NVL76 使單 GPU 對應 6 個 3.2T 光模組（需求 6 倍）；大立
光 Fiber Array、訊芯（光學組裝）、Ayar Labs 台積電鏈、光模組與零組件受惠。
• ASIC 設計服務：Google TPU（博通／聯發科）、AWS Trainium（Marvell／世芯）放量；聯發科在 V10
（Icefish）取得主導值得追蹤。
• 電源與散熱：800V/HD-VPC  提早導入，利多  PMIC、高壓  MOSFET、變壓元件（台達電等）、散熱冷板

（AVC、奇鋐、雙鴻）。
循環性／指數部位（控管乖離、嚴設停利）
• SOX 評價（約 30x PE、PB 創高）處循環高點，指數型部位避免追高，留意乖離回檔風險。
• 下游伺服器/網通/ODM 庫存偏高、正去化；PC 的 DDR5 屬被動拉貨備貨，留意下半年拉貨節奏與真空。
• 關鍵追蹤訊號：庫存天數是否由去化轉為失控、CSP  自由現金流與資本支出指引、ASIC  良率進度（牽動
NVIDIA 利多/利空）。
一句話總結
需求與變現方向明確向上，續抱『先進封裝＋光通訊/CPO/XO＋ASIC  設計服務＋電源散熱』四大主線；客戶離不開
台積電是最強的結構性護城河。但 SOX 已貴、下游庫存偏高——題材逢回布局優於追高，緊盯庫存去化與獲利兌現。

產品組合

股價相對大盤走勢

聯電 (2303 TT)  Buy

新產品帶動中長期成長，調升至 Buy，目標價至 168 元。  
投資建議 ：展望 2026 年，聯電認為 AI 相關領域仍將是 2026 年
半導體產業的主要成長驅動力。此外，隨著邊緣  AI 應用的持
續商業化部署，用於通用型伺服器的晶片需求預計也將復甦。
相比之下，記憶體供需失衡的負面影響可能會對部分消費性電
子產品帶來壓力。但整體而言，半導體產業預計在  2026 年將
YoY+14~16% 。晶圓代工市場預計成長約 YoY+20% ，預估聯電
成長將會高於所在市場的平均成長。預估聯電 2026 年稅後 EPS 
5.59 元。公司在先進封裝、矽光子及 Intel 合作的 12nm 陸續取
得進展，為  2027 年的中長期成長奠定基礎，有利業績向上。考
量聯電的  ASP 正處於從低谷回升並結構性轉強的階段，近期
市場對成熟製程晶圓代工產業評價上修，反映非中系成熟製程
之戰略價值提升 ， 因此調升至Buy， 目標價168 元 (2026PER30X) 。 
1Q26 稅後 EPS 1.29 元：聯電 1Q26 營收為 610.38 億元， QoQ-
1.25% 。聯電  1Q26 整體出貨量約當  12 吋晶圓  102.1 萬片，
QoQ+2.7% 。1Q26 產能利用率小幅增加至  79% 。ASP 因年初
價格調整而下滑。 1Q26 產能利用率微增至 79%，ASP QoQ-1% 。
1Q26 毛利率下滑 1.48 個百分點至 29.19% 。業外認列 53.67 億
元收益，來自利息收益 0.88 億元，匯兌收益 3.03 億元。認列投
資收益 49.97 億元 (約貢獻 30% 獲利，大都為手中持有的有價證
券評價，並未處份，後續視股市場波動 )，聯電 1Q26 稅後 EPS 
1.29 元。  
預估 2Q26 稅後 EPS 1.41 元：聯電預期通訊領域在 2Q26 將反
彈，消費性電子由  MCU 、 LCD 控制  IC 及電源相關產品等延
續成長力道。 預期 2H26 表現會優於 1H26， 主要是因為 HV（高
壓）、NVM（非揮發性記憶體） 、PMIC（電源管理  IC ）和 RFSOI 
等特殊製程專案將在 2H26 量產。預估聯電 2Q26 晶圓出貨量
QoQ+7~9% ， 2Q26 聯電產能利用率約 81~83% 。 ASP in USD 
QoQ+1~3% ，毛利率微增至 30% 左右。預估聯電 2Q26 稅後 EPS 
1.41 元。 1Q26 稅率偏低約 3%， 2Q26 用平均 15% 預估。

2 群益投顧 
  聯電 ESG ：  
聯電在 ESG 方面持續展現積極作為，並獲國內外多項評鑑肯定：  
永續策略與治理： ESG 由董事會與二位共同總經理組成的指導委員會全盤
規劃，持續以 「以人為本、與環境共生、與社會共榮」 為願景推動永續。 ESG
績效結合員工分紅與限制型股票條件，提升全員參與動能與企業凝聚力。  
環境面（ E）  
2025 年獲國際永續評鑑（如 FTSE Russell ）評為全台 ESG 表現頂尖企業，
半導體業唯一入選前 3。  
持續推動用水回收、能源效率提升，製程用水回收率在各廠均高於法規標
準。  
積極採購並投資離岸風電，與 CIP 渢妙風場簽 30 年綠電採購合約，展現對
2050 淨零承諾。  
社會面（ S）  
深化產學合作，與台灣 4 所大學半導體專班合作， 600 多位學生參與。  
啟動員工持股信託，落實多元薪酬政策，提升員工福祉並吸引人才。  
長期投入社會公益，如偏鄉關懷、青年培力、綠色創新等持續進行。  
公司治理（ G）  
董事會組成高度多元且獨立，獨董占三分之二、女性董事占三分之一。  
強化利害關係人溝通與資訊透明度，持續強化供應鏈 ESG 要求，並納入評
核與管理系統，推動價值鏈共榮。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

結論：2026 年營運成長雖無虞，惟 PER 未顯著低估

一、1Q26 毛利率、營益率低於預期，業外匯兌及利息
收入挹注，獲利達成率 129%，單季稅後 EPS 0.45 元 
仁寶 1Q26 營收 2,013 億元、+5%QoQ、+1%YoY，達
成率 111%，淡季不淡，優於預期，主要來自 AI Server
的挹注及 Smart Device 提前拉貨所致 ， 毛利率5.26%(原
估 5.65%)、-0.56ppts QoQ、+0.01ppts YoY，毛利率顯
著下滑，主要係受累記憶體漲價所致，營業利益 26.37
億元、持平 QoQ、-3%YoY，達成率 107%，營益率
1.31%(原估 1.36%)、QoQ -0.07ppts、YoY -0.05ppts，
受累毛利率顯著下滑所致 ， 業外淨收入4.47 億元優於預
期，包括有利息收入 2.59 億元及匯兌收益 4.83 億元，
抵銷權益法認列關聯聯企業及合資損失 2.65 億元所致，
稅前淨利 30.91 億元、+23%QoQ、-10%YoY，達成率
119%，稅後淨利 19.67 億元、+40%QoQ、-10%YoY，
獲利達成率 129%，單季稅後 EPS 0.45 元(原估 0.35
元)。 
5. 1Q26 營收+5%QoQ、+1%YoY， 產品營收比重PC NB
占 65%、-3%QoQ、-6%YoY，其中 NB 出貨量 5.9kk、
-13%QoQ、-16%YoY，出貨量雙位數季衰退，營收僅低
個位數下滑 ， 係因ASP 上漲及 AI PC 滲透率提升 ， 抵銷
部分出貨量下滑所致；Non PC 占 35%、+27%QoQ、
+18%YoY，其中 Non PC 中 Smart Device 有兩大客戶
約占營收 15%、智易占約 6-7%左右，其他車電、醫療
及 5G 模組占約 6~7%，Server 占約 5%左右的水準 。 其
中 Server 方面營收占 5%(2025 年僅 1~2%)，主要成長
來自 AI Server(HGX B300 L10)，Server 營收包括有
Server L6(MB)占約 1~2%，AI Server 則來到 4%左右的
水準。

二、展望2Q26，PC 季增低個位數，Non PC AI Server
仍是主要成長動能，推估季增約 10%，單季稅後 EPS 
0.45 元 
展望 2Q26，Non PC 中的 Server 仍是主要成長動能，
而 PC 出貨相較 1Q26 的傳統淡季 ，2Q26 出貨量可望有
低個位數季成長，因為CPU 短缺，產能不及供貨，致部
分訂單推延至 2Q26，加上 ASP 因漲價而持續向上，預
期營收季成長無虞，惟相較於 1Q26 時看法，客戶需求
有轉趨保守現象，原預期全年出貨量力拼年持平，將會
下修至與研調 IDC 看法類似(-11%YoY)，出貨量雙位數
年衰退 。 投顧預估2Q26 營收 2,214 億元 、+10%QoQ、
+23%YoY， 其中PC 季增約 5%、Non PC 季增約 20%，
仁寶公告 5 月營收 704 億元、-2%MoM、+22%YoY，季
營收達成率 64%， 累計營收+8%YoY， 其中NB 出貨 200
萬台、MoM 11%。2Q26 合併毛利率預期仍受原材料價
格上漲影響而小幅下滑至 5.2%、-0.06pptsQoQ、
-0.67pptsYoY，營益率 1.49%、+0.18pptsQoQ、
+0.04pptsYoY，稅後淨利 19.71 億元、持平 QoQ、
+308%YoY，單季稅後 EPS 0.45 元(原 0.46 元)。 
三、展望2026 年，PC 出貨年衰退雙位數，但受惠 ASP
提升，致 PC 營收力拼年增持平，非 PC 業務仍是主要
成長動能 ， 推升營收比重挑戰40%， 合併營收+22%YoY，
稅後 EPS 2.2 元  
展望 2026 年，PC 出貨原預期 YoY 力拼持平，但因近
期 CPU 缺貨至訂單出貨推延外 ， 觀察客戶需求亦有趨於

四、評等中立理由 
預估2026 年稅後 EPS 2.2 元 ， 同時預估2027 年 EPS 2.6
元，目前股價 37.1 元，PER 17X 及 14X 未顯著偏低，
仁寶目前正處於跨入 AI 領域的轉型期，受惠 Neocloud
客戶的 NV HGX 產品出貨，營運會有不錯的成長，惟目
前 RACK 系統的進度相對落後 Tier 1 的 ODM 供應商，
高階 RACK 系統進度仍受限廠房供電變數及認證進度，
停留在 HGX、MGX 等解決方案上 ， 主要客戶仍以Tier2、
Tier3 客戶為主，故營運變數高，不確定性仍大，故給予
中立投資評等，給予目標價 40 元(15X2027EPS)。

2327.TT 國巨* 
 Rubin 加持，進入千金股新時代！

[財務資訊更新]   
   公司簡介 
國巨*為國際級被動元件解決方案的大企業，目前在全球 32 國生產 6 大類別共計 82
種被動元件相關的產品線 ， 除了在AI 伺服器 、 汽車電子與工業應用等高成長市場占重
要地位之外，也透過轉投資與多家電源管理 IC 公司展開策略合作。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 1,275 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，858 
【大盤指數/股價】45809 / 950

➢ 2Q26 漲價與訂單外溢效應持續，正式進入超級循環起點 
國巨*目前整體營運在 AI 伺服器、高效能運算與車用需求帶動下持續升
溫，訂單與出貨比與稼動率皆有所上升，且客戶庫存亦低於公司認為的
健康水位。公司部分晶片電阻、磁性元件與鉭質電容皆跟進日本與韓國
等國際大廠調漲，再加上旗下用於手機與消費性電子等非 AI 應用的 22
μF 與 10μF 高容量 MLCC 也因國際大廠產能轉移 ， 而有望獲得更多訂單
外溢的效應。投研部預估國巨*2Q26 營收 451.77 億元，QoQ+18.4%，
YoY+37.9%，續創季度歷史新高。在漲價與產品組合優化的情況下，毛
利率 38.5%，季增 0.4ppts，年增 3ppts；營業利益率 27.3%，季增 2.1
個百分點，年增 5.8 個百分點；稅後淨利率 22.6%，季增 1.6 個百分點；
年增 7.3 個百分點；稅後 EPS 4.91 元，QoQ+26%、YoY+101.8%。 
➢ 旗下 4 大產品需求暴增，且電容供需缺口將延續至 2027 年 
目前因 AI 伺服器與電動車對高階 MLCC 需求激增，供需缺口顯著，日
韓多家大廠的 MLCC 價格已出現全面性上漲，而國巨*尚未對 MLCC 跟
進調漲，後續發展值得期待。此外，輝達(NVDA.US) Rubin 伺服器機櫃
的電容、磁性元件、電阻與感測器，等被動元件總價值(BOM 成本)將迎
來結構性的爆發，預計從 GB200 的 1.2 萬～1.5 萬美元翻 3 倍至 Vera 
Rubin 的 3.5 萬～4.5 萬美元左右。由於國巨*目前在輝達 L10 機櫃組裝
供應鏈中為少數能同時提供高階 MLCC、高功率電感以及鉭質電容的全
方位解決方案供應商，在新世代伺服器放量後將成為台廠主要受惠者，
投研部預估 2H26 至 2027 年能為國巨*帶來約 3.5 億～5 億美元的營收
增量。 
➢ 被動元件迎接結構性需求，給予買進投資建議，目標價 1,275 元 
投研部預估國巨*2026 年/2027 全年營收為 1,802.81 億元/2,333.19 億
元，YoY+35.6%/29.4%；在 AI 伺服器與車用電子領域的占比加重，且
產品歷經多次漲價 ， 再加上Vera Rubin 平台供應鏈的相關零組件門檻偏
高，將使毛利率上升至 38.7%/41%；營業利益率為 27.6%/31.4%；淨
利率為 22.5%/25.2%； 稅後EPS 19.59 元/28.32 元 。投研部考量公司長
期布局高階產品，相關產品與日本、韓國同業差距不大，因此給予買進
的投資操作建議，目標價 1,275 元(2027 年 PER 45X)。

個股報告

公司簡介 
國巨*透過併購，目前在全球 32 國營運，擁有53 座生產基地、35 個銷售服務據點及 20
個研發中心，旗下被動元件產品涵蓋 6 大類別，共計 82 種產品線，其中在晶片電組與
鉭電容的市占率第一，且在 MLCC 與功率電感的市占率為第三，在 AI 伺服器、汽車電
子與工業應用等高成長市場占重要地位。公司除了已經從單純的被動元件供應商升級為
零組件解決方案企業之外，也透過轉投資與富鼎 (8261)、力智(6719)與茂達(6138)展開
策略合作，以強化電源管理供應鏈布局與進一步提升 AI 資料中心滲透率。 
1Q26 展現強勁的 AI 增長動能，營收與獲利創新高並優於預期 
國巨*1Q26 營收 381.66 億元，QoQ+6.1%，YoY+22.7%，創季度歷史新高，優於投研
部預期。其中，積層陶瓷電容(MLCC)與鉭質電容的占比有所增加，分別由 17.7%上升至
19%，以及由 21.7%上升至 24.3%；另感測器也由 11.2%增加至 13%。營收成長動能除
了來自 AI 相關需求強勁，使其營收佔比自 4Q25 的 12%～13%提高 2 個百分比至
14%~15%之外，國巨*調漲部分標準品報價也發揮了正面效益。因上述理由產生產品組
合優化的情況下，營業毛利 145.42 億元，QoQ+8.4%、YoY+31.2%；毛利率 38.1%，
季增 0.8ppts，年增 2.5ppts；營業利益 96.13 億元，QoQ+10.1%、YoY+48.7%，為
2021 年第 4 季以來高點；營業利益率 25.2%，季增 0.9 個百分點，年增 4.4 個百分點；
稅後淨利 80.01 億元，QoQ+18.5%、YoY+44.7%；稅後淨利率 21%，季增 2 個百分
點；年增 3.1 個百分點；稅後 EPS 3.9 元，QoQ+18.52%、YoY+44.69%。

資料來源：國巨*法說會簡報、國票投顧整理 
2Q26 漲價與訂單外溢效應持續，正式進入超級循環起點 
國巨*目前整體營運在 AI 伺服器、高效能運算與車用需求帶動下持續升溫，在訂單與出
貨比(BB Ratio)方面，公司整體為 1.3，AI 相關產品高達 1.4(對比 4 月 15 日法說會上曾
提及各終端市場均維持於 1 以上)；在稼動率方面，一般標準品約 78%、高階特殊品約
88%(對比 4 月 15 日法說會上曾提及一般品為 70%～72%、 高階特殊品為80%～82%)，
皆較 1Q26 上升；在客戶庫存方面，國巨*追蹤的全球主要代理商庫存持續下降，已低於
公司認為健康水位的 5.2 個月；觀察具指標性的個別大廠，因蘇州廠位於大中華區消費
性產品的元件生產中心，目前產能出現罕見滿載，呈現出 2018 年被動元件超級循環後
便未曾出現的盛況。公司部分晶片電阻、磁性元件與鉭質電容皆跟進日本與韓國等國際
大廠調漲 ， 再加上旗下用於手機與消費性電子等非AI 應用的 22μF 與 10μF 高容量 MLCC
也因國際大廠產能轉移，而有望獲得更多訂單外溢的效應 。

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
投研部預估國巨*2Q26 營收 451.77 億元，QoQ+18.4%，YoY+37.9%，續創季度歷史
新高。在漲價與產品組合優化的情況下，營業毛利 174.08 億元， QoQ+19.7% 、
YoY+49.4%；毛利率 38.5%，季增 0.4ppts，年增 3ppts；營業利益 123.22 億元，
QoQ+28.2%、YoY+74.9%；營業利益率 27.3%，季增 2.1 個百分點，年增 5.8 個百分
點 ； 稅後淨利102.21 億元 ，QoQ+27.2%、YoY+103.9%； 稅後淨利率22.6%，季增 1.6
個百分點；年增 7.3 個百分點；稅後 EPS 4.91 元，QoQ+26%、YoY+101.8%。 
Rubin 對旗下 4 大項產品需求暴增，且電容供需缺口將延續至 2027 年 
因 AI 伺服器與電動車對高階 MLCC 需求激增，而高階產線建設週期較長，即使將傳統
低階 MLCC 生產線改建也需 3 個月的工程期，短期產能難以快速釋放，供需缺口顯著，
日韓多家大廠的 MLCC 價格已出現全面性上漲。此外，輝達計畫於 2H26 推出次世代
Vera Rubin NVL72平台 ， 在單一機架架構上帶來顯著的硬體革新與規格躍升 ，MLCC 因
產能吃緊，其報價可望持續調漲，而國巨*尚未對 MLCC 跟進調漲，後續發展值得期待。 
隨著每片 Compute Board 升級搭載採用 3 奈米製程的 Rubin GPU，整體機櫃熱設計功
耗(TDP)將會由 GB200 的 120kW～140kW 大幅提升至 180kW～230kW，受惠於運算
效能與供電需求同步擴張 ， 被動元件及各類感測器的配置數量也將明顯增加 。 此外 ，Vera 
Rubin 首度導入無纜線、無水管、無風扇的 PCB Mid -plane(中央背板)設計，進一步提
升系統整合度與空間利用效率。受此架構演進帶動，單片板材上的電容與電阻使用量預
計較 Blackwell 世代增加 50%~100%， 反映出新平台在高密度供電與訊號管理方面的更
高需求。 
若以最主流的 NVL72(亦即包含 36 片運算板/18 個運算托盤、1 組主要網路托盤系統，
以及 6 組標準電源機櫃托盤)規格進行全機櫃四大核心組件的用量加總 ， 則各大類被動元
件從 GB200 轉變為 Vera Rubin 平台的總用量與增幅如下(1)電容 ：Rubin 因 3 奈米製程
及 HBM4 電流需求，MLCC 與高階鉭電容用量接近翻倍，使電容用量將從 GB200 的 29
萬~44 萬顆增為 66 萬~81 萬顆，世代增幅約 84%~120%；(2)磁性元件：Rubin 全面
導入更密集的 TLVR 雙迴路電感與高功率電源大電感，使磁性元件用量將從 GB200 的
7,500 至 11,550 顆增為 15,080 至 20,880 顆，世代增幅約 80%；(3)電阻：高頻訊號匹
配與阻抗控制，帶動精密薄膜電阻用量暴增，使電阻用量將從GB200 的 37 萬~56 萬顆
增為 74 萬~95 萬顆，世代增幅約 70%~100%；(4)感測器：Rubin 功耗飆升至 200kW
等級，需要 2 倍的溫控點與全方位的液冷漏液、高壓安全偵測晶片，使感測器用量將從
GB200 的 850~1,330 顆增為 1,700~2,365 顆，世代增幅約 77%~100%。因此，伺服
器機櫃的被動元件總價值(BOM 成本)將迎來結構性的爆發 ， 預計從GB200 的 1.2 萬~1.5
萬美元翻 3 倍至 Vera Rubin 的 3.5 萬~4.5 萬美元左右。 
由於國巨*目前在輝達 L10 機櫃組裝供應鏈中為少數能同時提供高階 MLCC、高功率電
感以及鉭質電容的全方位解決方案供應商，因此投研部預估原本在 GB200 世代的單一
機櫃被動元件產值約 1.2 萬~1.5 萬美元當中，若扣除其他電感廠份額，則國巨*約可獲
得 6,000～8,000 美元；待 Rubin 平台放量後，單機產值將因大量採用單價高出數十倍
的 X6S 與 X7S 等高壓高容值的頂規 MLCC 以及更大量的鉭質電容而暴增至 3.5 萬～4.5
萬美元，國巨*在單櫃的營收產值將大幅跳升至 1.5 萬～2 萬美元。若以市場預估 2025
年~2026 年 GB200 系列出貨約 4～5 萬台，再加上 2026 下半年起 Vera Rubin 世代初
步進入量產並陸續放量的規模來計算 ， 來自前者將貢獻約2.4 億～3.5 億美元的營收 ， 而
即使首批 Rubin 出貨量僅約 GB200 的一半，在單櫃價值翻倍的情況下，也能為國巨*帶
來約 3.5 億～5 億美元的營收。

瑞昱 (2379 TT)  Trading Buy

1H26 客戶有提前拉貨 ， 瑞昱營運淡季不淡 ， 升評為Tradin g 
Buy。  
投資建議 ：因記憶體價格上漲， 1H26 客戶有提前拉貨，瑞昱營
運淡季不淡； AI 會帶動網通產品規格提升，瑞昱也會受惠，升
評為 Trading Buy 。  
瑞昱 1Q26 獲利大幅季增 ：瑞昱 1Q26 營收 364.23 億元，
QoQ+38.60%/YoY+4.00% ；稅後純益 43.30 億元，
QoQ+63.15%/YoY-9.07% ；稅後 EPS 8.40 元。獲利大幅季增，因
記憶體價格上漲，客戶有提前備貨，瑞昱表示，拉貨動能延續
至 2Q26 。  
瑞昱 2H26 營運展望較保守 ：瑞昱表示， 2H26 供應鏈不確定性
仍高，尤其記憶體價格仍維持高檔，加上 CPU 、封裝及運輸 …
等成本同步墊高，品牌廠自 2Q26 起陸續調漲 PC 售價，後續終
端需求能否順利承接，仍需持續觀察。  
瑞昱有信心 PC 業務可成長 ：研調機構之前表示 2026 年全球 PC
出貨量衰退 2.4% ，但近期更新為衰退 11% ，瑞昱 1Q26 產品應
用比： PC 36% 、非 PC 64% 。  
瑞昱仍對 PC 業務成長仍具信心，主要成長來源是市佔率提升
與單機半導體含量增加 。 隨著 Wi-Fi 7 、2.5G/10G Ethernet 、USB、
Audio 與 Video… 等規格持續升級 ， 帶動高階連接晶片需求成長 ，
也有助提升產品平均售價與附加價值。  
群益預估瑞昱 2026 年 /2027 年稅後 EPS 30.54 元 /37.00 元：群
益預估瑞昱 2026 年 /2027 年營收為 1,362.04 億元 /1,566.35 億
元，YoY+11.00%/+15.00% ；稅後純益 157.42 億元 /190.75 億元，
YoY+6.70%/+21.18% ；稅後 EPS 30.54 元 /37.00 元。

資料來源：公司，群益投顧彙整。  
瑞昱 1Q26 有多項成長動能：  
瑞昱指出， 1Q26 成長動能主要來自 AI 基礎建設需求，包括高階路由器、交
換器及 Wi-Fi 7 產品，同時高階電視、遊戲 … 等消費電子產品及車用市場亦
維持穩健，不過記憶體價格上漲、上游供應鏈緊張及地緣政治風險，可能壓
抑 2Q26 成長動能。  
瑞昱有信心 PC 業務可成長：  
研調機構之前表示 2026 年全球 PC 出貨量衰退 2.4% ，但近期更新為衰退

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 6 月 11 日

3 群益投顧 
11% ，瑞昱 1Q26 產品應用比： PC 36% 、非 PC 64% 。  
瑞昱仍對 PC 業務成長仍具信心，主要成長來源是市佔率提升與單機半導體
含量增加。隨著 Wi-Fi 7 、 2.5G/10G Ethernet 、 USB 、 Audio 與 Video… 等規
格持續升級，帶動高階連接晶片需求成長，也有助提升產品平均售價與附加
價值。  
瑞昱預期 2026 年 PC WiFi 7 滲透率可望達 30% ：  
網通晶片方面， Wi-Fi 7 滲透率提升，相對也同步推升瑞昱網通晶片出貨成
長。瑞昱預期 2026 年 PC WiFi 7 滲透率可望達 30%，並帶動傳輸規格升級，
挹注需求動能。  
世足賽帶動電視換機潮：  
消費性電子方面， 2026 年世足賽將帶動 OLED 、 LED 電視的換機潮，加上
Switch 2 的推出，可望帶動相關需求。  
AI 基礎建設帶動網通需求：  
瑞昱表示， AI 快速成長為 2H26 網通市場帶來上行機會，超大型 CSP 正從
AI 實驗邁向工業規模 AI 工廠，企業端也開始重建資料中心，帶動 800G 與
1.6T Ethernet 交換器需求升溫。

  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

2404 漢唐(UIS)

報告日期： 2026/06/12  研 究 員：翁浩軒 (Davy Wong) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 半導體大客戶海內外擴產持續 
漢唐為台灣半導體大廠之長期配合廠務工程公司，近年隨半導體
需求強勁，半導體廠於全球建廠，推動漢唐工程案大幅成長，國
內大客戶於新竹、台中、高雄持續建置 2nm 及 2nm 以下之先進製
程，海外方面，美國 Fab21 P2 已完工過半，預估 2026 年底認完 9
成，儘管美國廠建廠成本為台灣數倍，然漢唐已有建廠經驗，在
人力調配、成本控管上掌握度更高，在工程效率有所提升，預估
接下來美國廠建廠之毛利率有望回升。

二. 1Q26 毛利率優於預期，EPS 16.17 元 
漢唐 1Q26 營收 202.88 億(QoQ-1.3% 、YoY+76.15%) ，受惠於建廠需
求強勁，營收淡季不淡，毛利率提升至 23.32%優於預期，主要因
工程執行效率提升，並反應之前預提之成本，稅後淨利 30.81 億
(QoQ+16.51%、YoY+54.88%)，EPS 16.17 元。

三. 2026 年毛利率改善，預估全年 EPS 85.15 元 
展望 2026 年 ， 公司至 115 年 5 月之已簽約未認列之金額 1939 億元 ，
創歷史新高 ， 預估隨著客戶 P3 陸續下單 ， 未認列金額將持續上升 。
台灣市場 Foundry 大客戶建廠持續 ， 而美系記憶體廠亦有統包案挹
助; 海外部分，美國 P2 陸續認列營收至年底，並開始 P3 之建廠，
而美國之工案因反應成本單價較高，而在與下包商配合後，執行
效率提升，且因客戶積極推進工程進度，帶動美國建廠之毛利率
提升。預估漢唐 2026 年營收 1072.97 億(YoY+62.34%)，稅後淨利
162.28 億(YoY+78.93%)，EPS 85.15 元。預估 2027 年營收 1446.97
億(YoY+34.86%) ， 稅後淨利 227.22 億(YoY+40.01%) ， EPS 119.22 元 。

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 1550 元 
投資建議方面，漢唐為專業半導體廠務工程公司，營運表現高度
連動半導體資本支出及半導體廠之新建廠計劃，受惠於 AI 需求帶
動先進製程、記憶體供不應求，半導體無塵室已成為市場緊缺資
產，在全球廠務工程供不應求下，接單價格亦優於過往，漢唐在
半導體客戶海內外擴產持續下訂單無虞。考量到公司訂單能見度
提高 、 今明年營收獲利創高 ， 參考 2027 年 EPS 給予 13 倍本益比，
目標價 1550 元，建議買進。

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
漢唐成立於 1982 年 9 月，主要業務為服務高科技產業之建廠及擴
廠計劃，業務範圍包括半導體、光電、封裝測試、太陽能、LED、
生技等產業。公司為專業提供高科技產業建廠之系統集成服務公
司；主要業務範圍包括技術顧問服務、高科技整廠集成專案及電
腦、控制、無塵室、機電、電信系統之專案業務。2025 年營收比
重為系統整合佔 99.5%、維護服務佔 0.1%、設計業務/產品銷售占
0.4%。公司主要客戶包括台積電、友達、日月光、力晶、南科、
旺宏、泰林、彩晶、華邦電、南茂、精材、南京熊貓、華星光電、
深超光電、美光等工程訂單。無塵室及機電統包工程競爭者：帆
宣、同開、漢科、聖暉、擎邦、亞翔、千附。

二.2026 年營運概況及展望  
1. 半導體大客戶海內外擴產持續 
漢唐為台灣半導體大廠之長期配合廠務工程公司，近年隨半導體
需求強勁、全球供應鏈重組，帶動半導體廠於全球建廠，亦推動
漢唐工程案大幅成長，其中漢唐大客戶先進製程供不應求，近年
於國內外續擴建新廠，國內部分，大客戶於新竹、台中、高雄持
續建置 2nm及 2nm以下之先進製程 ， 將挹助漢唐訂單能見度達 2030
年; 另因大客戶至美國設廠，漢唐在一廠設置時即配合大客戶於

2020 年 11 月成立美國分公司，並於 2022~2023 年挹助營收大幅成
長，大客戶美國 AZ Fab21 P1 已開始量產，P2 目前施作中，已完
工過半，預估 2026 年底認完 9 成，客戶 P3 已小量下單、P4 規劃
中 ， 而 Fab21 為超大規模的先進製程聚落 ， 總計將建置 6 座晶圓廠 、
2 座先進封裝廠及 1 個研發中心，預期漢唐未來五年接單無虞。儘
管當初因為美國廠建廠成本為台灣數倍，加上人工、材料成本，
法規等影響使得建廠成本較原先預期為高，然漢唐已有上次建廠
經驗，在人力調配、成本控管上掌握度更高，在工程效率有所提
升，加上也會跟客戶反應高漲之成本，因此預估接下來美國廠建
廠之毛利率有望回升。

2. 1Q26 毛利率優於預期，EPS 16.17 元 
漢唐 1Q26 營收 202.88 億(QoQ-1.3% 、YoY+76.15%) ，受惠於建廠需
求強勁，營收淡季不淡，毛利率提升至 23.32%優於預期，主要因
工程執行效率提升，並反應之前預提之成本，稅後淨利 30.81 億
(QoQ+16.51%、YoY+54.88%)，EPS 16.17 元。

3. 2Q26 營收雙位數季增，預估 EPS 19.6 元 
2Q26 受惠於台灣、美國先進製程建廠持續，尤其是美國 P2 貢獻
下，營收將續創新高，5 月營收 9 2 . 1 8 億( M o M + 1 3 . 7 %、
YoY+85.01%) ， 預 估 2Q26 營 收 262.94 億 (QoQ+29.6% 、
YoY+77.84%)，毛利率 21.5%，稅後淨利 37.36 億(QoQ+21.25%、
YoY+167.22%)，EPS 19.6 元。

4. 2026 年毛利率改善，預估全年 EPS 85.15 元 
展望 2026 年 ， 公司至 115 年 5 月之已簽約未認列之金額 1939 億元 ，
創歷史新高 ， 預估隨著客戶 P3 陸續下單 ， 未認列金額將持續上升 。
台灣市場包含新竹、台中、高雄之半導體建廠持續，而美系記憶
體廠亦有統包案挹助; 海外部分，美國 P2 陸續認列營收至年底，
並開始 P3 之建廠，P4 及先進封裝廠預估將於明年下半年開始貢
獻，而美國之工案因反應成本單價較高，而在與下包商配合後，
執行效率提升，且因客戶積極推進工程進度，帶動美國建廠之毛
利率提升。預估漢唐 2026 年營收 1072.97 億(YoY+62.34%)，毛利
率 22.39%，稅後淨利 162.28 億(YoY+78.93%)，EPS 85.15 元。預估
2027 年營收 1446.97 億(YoY+34.86%)，毛利率 22.86%，稅後淨利
227.22 億(YoY+40.01%)，EPS 119.22 元。

產品組合

股價相對大盤走勢

全新 (2455 TT)  Buy

考量營收將因基板料況改善而重拾動能，評等調整為 Buy。 
投資建議 ：由於中國政府 05/2026 月底放行 2026 年首批磷化銦
基板出口，全新光電子產品出貨表現受限缺料的狀況隨之改善，
考量 AI data center 衍生商機龐大，後續光電子產品營收成長動
能可期，故群益將全新的投資建議調整為 Buy 。  
中國政府放行 2026 年首批磷化銦基板出口： 全新 01/2026 營收
3.40 億元，但 02~05/2026 單月營收卻均未超過 3.3 億元，群益
認為，光電子產品出貨表現受磷化銦基板缺料影響為關鍵。不
過，中 國 政 府05/2026 月底放行 2026 年首批磷化銦基板出口 (前
次放行時間為 08/2025) ，預估全新光電子產品營收表現將自
06/2026 開始受惠於此，營收展望隨之轉佳。  
2026 年營收動能主要來自光電子產品： 展望 2026 年，光電子產
品營收成長性仍佳，全新資本支出主要用於添購 MOCVD 機台，
即係考量資料中心衍生商機逐年擴大。其中，光電子接收端產
品大客戶為台系晶圓代工廠，目前市佔率居同業之冠，雖然產
品認證進度近期傳出較多雜音，但光通訊產業景氣向上，預估
該客戶仍為重要受惠者；另一大客戶為美系 IC 設計公司，近期
釋出給全新的訂單數量增加幅度較大，預估 2026 年對全新貢獻
的營收成長性將可期。其次，全新光電子發射端產品 4Q24 已量
產，主要供應給日系客戶， 2025 年各季訂單數量均符合先前預
估，且 2026 年成長幅度具想像空間，全新除增加用於相關訂單
的 MOCVD 機台外，亦已為此添購 e-beam 機台等週邊設備。  
至於在微電子產品的部份，雖然近幾年全新的中國市佔率逐年
提高，不過，現階段手機產業景氣受到記憶體等零組件供應吃
緊影響，各家品牌業者對 2026 年出貨前景看法均相對謹慎，預
估全新微電子產品營收僅與 2025 年接近。

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就企業溫室氣體排放量減量目標而言，由於全新專注於製造、銷售及研發化
合物半導體磊晶片，溫室氣體排放量多寡，主要和能源耗用、產能及產品組
成有關。全新乃就單位產品的能源耗用與溫室氣體排放量，設定目標為每年
減少 5%，並將視產能擴充與產品變化情形，賡續追蹤與調整設定與目標。
至於企業產品或服務帶給客戶或消費者之減碳效果的部份，全新為客戶代
工生產的產品多數運用於通訊等電子產品，非屬終端消費性產品；全新除協
助客戶設計低耗能、高效率產品，以降低產品生命週期的資源耗用外，亦持
續進行生產製造過程的節能與減廢，以降低對環境所造成的衝擊，減少單位
產品所消耗的資源與污染物，為客戶及社會創造減碳環保效益。  
Forward PE Band

5 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Update
WinWay Technology Co Ltd  | Asia Pacific
May sales came in better than
feared; OW
What's new? Winway reported May sales of NT$1,073mn, +8.5%M/M and +120%Y/
Y, better than buyside expectations of flat M/M. This was mainly helped by the
gradual ramp of new socket capacity and strong demand  from NVIDIA AI GPU/CPU,
AMD CPU, and Google TPU, although probe card contribution was lower.
Stay OW: We continue to expect 2Q revenue growth of 15-20% Q/Q, implying

strong June sales of +40% M/M, as new socket capacity comes fully  online to
support the strong backlog and incremental MEMS probe card revenue. The share
price has pulled back  amid investor concerns over weak May sales, which we see as
a good entry point  at 42x 2026e EPS.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
WinWay Technology Co Ltd (6515.TW)
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture
long-term value. We assume a 9.14% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium
5.5%), a payout ratio of 80%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.4%, and a terminal growth rate
of 4.5%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected adoption of hypersockets and MEMS probe cards

n Faster-than-expected SL T and burn-in socket introduction
n Stronger-than-expected edge AI demand
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected adoption of hypersockets and MEMS probe cards
n Slower-than-expected SL T and burn-in socket introduction
n No edge AI demand in the next few years

Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 1

Largan Precision Co Ltd 
Downgrade to Neutral; As good as it gets

After Largan’s 50% share price rise in the past 1M (45% outperformance vs Taiex), 
we downgrade to Neutral, as we think the company’s growth potential from 
variable aperture lens upgrade and solid iPhone demand has been priced in, and the 
market has somewhat turned too positive on its co -packaged optics (CPO) 
progress. Longer term, we still like the company’s efforts into CPO, as this is a new 
addressable market for Largan and would require Largan’s expertise in precision 
requirements. However, we think there are still uncertainties on how Largan could 
turn this opportunity into real earnings (at least not in the next 1 -2 years, in our 
view) given the high competition and the incompatibility of Largan’s FAU product 
with the current chip design ecosystem; we suggest waiting on the sidelines for 
more concrete progress. Our new 2026E/27E EPS are still 7%/5% above BBGe 
due to better iPhone demand estimates, but we think valuation would become 
demanding for further re-rating (currently already trading at 20x 12M fwd P/E, 
above historical avg. of 16-17x) as CPO is at least 1.5-2 years away for Largan. 
• Silicon Photonics or CPO. Although Largan declined to comment, we believe 
the company is trying to make the Fiber Array Unit (FAU) with in-house 
components for IC design vendors, based on our latest industry checks. 
According to the Chairman’s public comments at the AGM yesterday (link), 
Largan will invite potential customers to the fab in September to see Largan’s 
products. In our view, this indicates that no customer has signed a contract or 
MOU with Lagan’s FAU to date. We agree that Largan's strength could be its 
solid manufacturing capabilities with high precision requirements, while a 
weakness could be its lack of track record and customer relationships as a 
newcomer into silicon photonics. Lastly, Largan showcased its FAU during the 
latest Computex, which we consider very innovative. However, this design is 
not compatible with the current ecosystem of chip design. Thus, Largan will 
need IC design companies to change/adjust/create ICs that can be compatible 
with its FAU. This leads us to believe it will take a long time for its FAU 
business to bear fruit, even if customers agree to do so. 
• iPhone lens upgrade. Largan is a major beneficiary of the upcoming iPhone 
camera upgrade to the iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max, as the variable aperture 
camera adoption will be one of the highlights. We estimate Largan’s content 
could see a meaningful increase (including lens and blade) compared to the 
main camera of the iPhone 17 Pro and Pro Max. However, we don’t think the 
foldable iPhone would benefit Largan much, as there will be no periscope 
camera in it, and the rear camera will still pop up (less precision requirement). 
• Implications. We increase our EPS estimates by 8% and 12% in 2026E and 
2027E to factor in stronger iPhone demand vs our previous assumptions. 
However, we downgrade our rating to Neutral as we believe these have been 
priced in, and its CPO progress is still too early to see any real contribution. Our 
new Dec-26 PT of NT$3,600 is still based on a 17x 2027E P/E, in line with the 
average P/E since 2012. We believe it is too early to call a share price re-rating. 
Asia Pacific Equity Research 
10 June 2026

Key Metrics (FYE Dec)  
Summary Investment Thesis and Valuation  
Investment Thesis 
We have a Neutral rating on the shares, as we think the 
company’s stable sales growth from the variable aperture lens 
upgrade and resilient iPhone demand has been priced in, and the 
market has somewhat turned too positive on its CPO progress. 
We think there are still uncertainties on how quickly Largan 
could turn this opportunity into real earnings (at least not in the 
next 1-2 years) given the high competition and the company’s 
lack of track record (vs comps), so we would suggest waiting on 
the sidelines for more concrete progress.

Investment Thesis, Valuation and Risks 
Largan Precision Co Ltd (Neutral; Price Target: NT$3,600.00) 
Investment                                  Thesis  
We have a Neutral rating on the shares, as we think the company’s stable sales growth from 
the variable aperture lens upgrade and resilient iPhone demand has been priced in, and the 
market has somewhat turned too positive on its CPO progress. We think there are still 
uncertainties on how quickly Largan could turn this opportunity into real earnings (at least 
not in the next 1-2 years) given the high competition and the company’s lack of track record 
(vs comps), so we would suggest waiting on the sidelines for more concrete progress. 
Valuation 
Our Dec-26 PT of NT$3,600 is based on a 17x 2027E P/E, in line with the average P/E since 
2012. 
Risks to Rating and Price Target 
Key upside risks to our rating and price target include: 1) faster progress in the new CPO/ 
FAU business; and 2) better-than-feared smartphone demand.

Key downside risks to our rating and price target include: 1) a much-faster-than-expected 
loss of market share to rivals, especially in the high-end lens market; 2) weaker smartphone 
sell-through; and 3) slower camera lens spec migration.

Epiwafer price hikes
Double-digit increases for GaAs and InP from June
VPEC announced a price hike on epiwafers, with both GaAs and InP to see double-
digit increases from June. This is the second time it has raised prices this quarter,
which strengthens our confidence in a better margin recovery ahead. We still
expect substrate supply to improve in coming quarters, with sales to regain

momentum from June, followed by double-digit sequential growth in 3Q26. We
remain positive on VPEC due to share gain opportunities in EML/CW lasers into
next year and structural margin improvement, and our 2027/28 EPS forecasts are
36%/26% above consensus. Maintain Outperform with TP of NT$465.
q VPEC announces epiwafer price hike: Prices for both GaAs and InP epiwafers will
increase by double digits from June (with greater hikes for InP) to reflect higher input

costs. This is the second time VPEC has raised epiwafer prices this quarter, which
strengthens our confidence in a better margin recovery from 2Q26.
q Sales momentum to pick up from June: While QTD sales are tracking slightly behind
our forecast of +10% QoQ due to substrate shortages, we believe momentum will
pick up from June onwards, followed by double-digit QoQ growth in 3Q26, backed by
additional MOCVD capacity, better substrate supply and stronger backlog orders.

q Key beneficiary of InP shortages: VPEC is in a unique position as a third-party
epiwafer house, which allows it to benefit from the growing outsourcing trend among
global optical chip leaders, and it is likely to gain 2-3 new clients for EML/CW lasers in
early 2027. We expect VPEC to outperform the sector growth in coming years.
q Maintain Outperform: Our 2027/28 EPS forecasts are 36%/26% above consensus as
we expect stronger optical growth with better margins. Trading at 28x 27SP EPS

versus the sector average of 48x, we see better risk/reward in VPEC’s share price, and
expect the improved sales momentum from June to help it catch up. Our target price
of NT$465 is based on 40x 27SP PE and we reiterate an Outperform rating.

Valuation details
We use PE as our valuation methodology as we believe the market will primarily
focus on the earnings growth outlook, which is better reflected with PE metrics.
The stock has traded largely between -1sd (20x) and +1sd (40x) for the past seven
years (after the structural rerating since 2017 on optical and China localisation

growth) based on each phase of the industry cycle. We assign an upcycle PE
multiple of 40x to reflect a stronger optical outlook.

Date: 2026-06-15

### Page 1

ab 15 June 2026
Global Research
Elan Microelectronics
Spec upgrades and AI initiatives  support growth
Sustained revenue growth despite softer notebook shipments
We assume coverage of Elan at Buy with a PT of NT$188 (previously NT$139), based on
16x 2027E PE (prior 15x 2025E) post Q1 analyst meeting. Despite softer NB unit demand
(UBSe -9% YoY)  in 2026 on higher memory cost and CPU constraints, we believe Elan
can sustain sales growth at 9% YoY in 2026E on touchpad spec-upgrades, higher

premium/commercial mix for NB, and new opportunities in AI. We expect sales and EPS
growth to accelerate in 2027-28E for +12%/+23% YoY, respectively, on a PC demand
recovery; content growth from larger size and haptic adoption for touchpads, as well as
fingerprint sensors and touch  ICs; and  growth from AI business (16% of sales by 2028E
vs 5% in 2025). We think accelerating revenue growth and GM recovery  could support
potential OPM expansion, and earnings and valuation upside.

Spec/content upgrades supporting ASP expansion and GM recovery
We believe Elan is well positioned to monetise HMI (Human Machine Interface) spec and
content upgrades, which should drive both ASP and unit growth, outperforming overall
NB shipments. We expect its NB-related business (touchpad, touch IC, point-stick, and
fingerprint sensors), which collectively accounts for ~90% of sales, to see an 8% CAGR
over 2025-28E (vs -2% CAGR for NB unit shipments) on touchpad shifting to larger size

and rising penetration of haptic touchpads,  growth of touch  IC as technology shifts
from DLOC (Double Layer On-Cell) to SLOC (Single Layer On-Cell), and rising adoption
of fingerprint sensors. New features such as  stylus,  waterproof,  and glove-operable
were added to its touch solutions in recent years, as well as  lower power consumption
designs should drive an ASP uplift. Given rising foundry and OSAT costs, Elan is also
raising its pricing from Q326, and we believe this  should support a GM recovery.

AI set to become a new driver from 2027
Elan has been expanding into AI-related applications, including edge-AI such as smart-
city, ADAS, and drone, as well as aiming to supply EIC (Electronic IC) for   NPO (Near-
Packaged Optics)/CPO (Co-Packaged Optics) through partnering with PETA. Although
the majority of AI-related sales in H126 were exposed to smart-city and ADAS, we
expect sales for drone to see a meaningful pick-up from H226 as it ramps its integrated

solutions such as AI-box, camera module, touchpad and miniLED controller unit, lifting
ASP from US$10-30 for a single camera module to  over-US$300 for AI-box plus camera
module. We forecast its AI-business to see a 49% sales CAGR in 2025-30E, excluding
the potential revenue contribution from EIC, outpacing its traditional HMI business.
Valuation: Assume coverage at Buy with a NT$188 price target
Our PT of NT$188 is based on 16x 2027E P/E, the upcycle average after 2020. We lower

2026E EPS by 9% on softer NB demand, but raise 2027E/28E EPS by 4%/12% on
content growth for touch business and AI opportunities. We believe the share price does
not fully reflect the improving earnings profile and potential AI opportunities.

Elan Microelectronics UBS ResearchElan (2458.TW)
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Can Elan grow its  revenue amid softer PC/NB demand?
Likely. We expect Elan to sustain its leading position for NB human machine interface and  grow its
content value on increasing adoption of haptic touchpad, rising fingerprint sensor penetration, and

more controller IC required as touch screen technology shifts from DLOC  toward SLOC for lower total
cost, despite the overall NB market facing a contraction in 2026E as higher memory pricing and CPU
shortages could dampen demand. Elan is also expanding into AI-related applications including smart-
city, ADAS, drone, and EIC for NPO/CPO. We forecast 2026/27E sales to grow 9%/16% YoY, vs UBSe
global NB unit growth of -9.5% and +1.7%, respectively.

Q: Can Elan sustain or further expand its profitability?
Likely. We believe mix shift toward higher dollar content and better margin product lines could
support Elan's profit expansion, while OPEX increase should be slower than top-line growth, leading
to operating leverage. We also believe Elan will be able to reflect foundry and OSAT cost hikes to its
customers as NB HMI suppliers have consolidated in the past few years. Elan's GM recovered to
48.6% in 2025 from 45.0% in 2023, despite topline being similar. Although GM is likely to contract

YoY in 2026E to 47.4% on foundry/OSAT price hikes in H126 and less favourable mix, we believe its
GM should recover from H226 as it adjusts ASPs and ramps higher-GM products.  We forecast Elan's
GM to expand to  48.1% in 2027E and 48.6% in 2028E, while OPM should see faster expansion from
23.3% in 2026E to 25.5% in 2027E and 26.7% in 2028E on operating leverage.
We assume coverage at Buy with a PT of NT$188 (from NT$139), based on 16x 2027E PE (from 15x

2025E PE), the upcycle average after 2020 (vs 7-20x historical range). We believe Elan is a good proxy
for the NB replacement cycle and agentic AI PC entering 2027-28E and can deliver content growth in
ASP for its touch segment via migration to higher-end products such as larger size and haptic
touchpad, fingerprint sensor, and touch screen IC. Diversification into edge AI applications,
leveraging its expertise in HMI and sensing, could be a new growth driver from 2027, reducing its

reliance on the PC/NB cycle. We expect 2026 EPS of NT$9.42, up 11% YoY, with growth to accelerate
in 2027/28E with EPS of NT$11.76/13.79 as NB demand recovers and  AI product sales ramp up.
Elan is well positioned to maintain its HMI market share on its robust R&D for new features and cost-
effective solutions, when increasing its value-add through new product offerings. Its revenue was
broadly stable during 2023-25 but its GM expanded by 3.6ppt to  48.6% in 2025. This was due to

new product launches such as larger size touchpads, SLOC touch IC, and a more favourable mix. It
has been developing haptic touchpads for several years and recently penetrated into Microsoft's
Surface laptop with broadening adoption by other leading brands expected in the coming quarters.
The market generally associates Elan with NB demand given its roughly 90% sales exposure to that
segment, but we think investors could be overlooking the potential ASP uplift benefits coming from

HMI spec upgrades and new opportunities in the edge AI businesses. The stock is trading at 14x 12-
month fwd consensus EPS versus its historical 7-20x, below peer average. We believe the current
share price does not fully reflect the improving earnings profile and potential AI opportunities.

Elan Microelectronics UBS Research
Q126 results and outlook
Elan  held an analyst meeting on 8 June to review its Q126 result. The company also
discussed the business outlook, summarized the products it exhibited at Computex and
provided an update on its latest developments for AI-related projects. Q126 sales grew
13% QoQ to NT$3.23bn, in line with guidance of NT$3.2-3.4bn as pull-ins ahead of
memory inflation offset the traditional slow season. By key product lines, touchpad

accounted for 42% of Q126 sales (vs 45% in Q425), touch controller IC 22% (17%),
fingerprint sensor 13% (14%), and  point stick 13% (16%), while AI-related increased to
7% of Q126 sales from 6% in Q425.
Q126 GM  came in at 47.6%, down 1.7ppt QoQ due to less favourable mix, and rising
costs for raw materials and production. Its opex-to-sales ratio improved from 26.2% in
Q425 to 23.5% in Q126, supported by government R&D subsidy programs, driving
OpM to 24.2%. With non-op of NT$61m, Q126 EPS came in at NT$2.43.

For Q226, management guided a similar revenue range QoQ at NT$3.2–3.4bn with NB
rush orders likely to extend, and gross margin to remain stable at 46.5–49.5% as rising
foundry and OSAT costs create near-term margin pressure. Price adjustments are  under
discussion and are expected to gradually take effect from July 1, although a richer mix of

high-margin products should help mitigate the impact. OpM is guided at 21.4% to
25.9%, reflecting higher photomask and R&D expenses. We forecast Q2 sales to grow
2% QoQ to NT$32.8bn with stronger momentum from point-stick and AI segments,
while we expect  touchpad, touch screen IC, fingerprint IC, and MCU sales to be flattish
to down slightly QoQ. In terms of margins, we forecast GM to contract 0.2ppt QoQ to
47.4% on rising production cost and OPM of 22.9% (-1.2ppt QoQ) on higher opex.

Into H226, the PC market remains challenged due to ongoing supply constraints and
price increases across various components such as  memory and CPU. Despite the
uncertainty and potentially softer outlook, Elan indicated its customer order trends
remain broadly stable, noting that it expects revenue growth to be supported by several
upcoming product launches (including project wins in high-end Chromebook
(Googlebook)), rising penetration of touch screen and premium touchpad solutions for

top-5 notebook brands,   and RTX Spark expected for September debut. Elan’s recent
penetration into Microsoft’s Surface laptop represents a key validation milestone, which
we expect to drive broader adoption across other tier-one brands such as HP , Dell, and
Lenovo in their premium models, supporting ASP expansion from c.~US$3 for
traditional touchpads to ~US$10–15 for haptics. It also sees better demand from
commercial  over consumer customers, in line with UBS hardware team's expectations,

while brands are also aiming to launch more premium models including features such as
larger screen size, narrow border design and advanced touchpad to justify price hikes
amidst a higher component-cost environment.

Amid the rising cost of such components as CPU and memory, NB OEMs are aiming to
adopt cost-effective SLOC (single layer on-cell) touch display technology versus the
current DLOC (double layer on-cell) to further reduce the overall cost structure. As SLOC
only requires one layer of ITO sensor (vs 4 layers of ITO for DLOC) to detect a capacitance

change and to sense the point of touch, the production process is less complicated for
SLOC  than DLOC, although SLOC requires more touch controller ICs (3-4 chips for SLOC
vs 1 chip for DLOC) to ensure touch sensing accuracy, which is positive for Elan’s touch
IC business. But despite requiring more chips, SLOC touch panels still have a lower BOM
cost than DLOC given the less complicated panel production process, and we think the
advent of SLOC could be the catalyst to drive down touch display cost and push

adoption by NB brands, particularly under the current environment of rising BOM costs.
With overall NB demand expected to recover from 2027, we believe Elan’s touch IC
business should also recover after a softer 2026.

In terms of margins, we expect Elan's like-to-like GM to improve in H226, while overall
GM will depend on its product mix as the AI-business could carry lower-GM, despite
higher ASP and OP dollar.  OPEX is expected to remain largely stable, with spending still
R&D-driven. Management also aims to reallocate R&D  resources by shifting MCU
engineers to drone-related development,  and aims to apply more government R&D

subsidies to contain overall OPEX growth.
For 2026, we expect Elans's NB-related sales (touchpad, touch screen IC, fingerprint IC,
and point stick) to grow 3% YoY, vs overall NB shipment decline of 9% YoY, while we
expect non-NB sales (MCU and AI) to grow 71% YoY on accelerated growth in AI
business from a growing  drone business with higher shipments of AI box and integrated
camera modules. Net-net, we forecast Elan's 2026 revenue to grow 9% YoY with AI mix

increasing to 11% of total sales, from 5% in 2025.
AI business driven by increasing content in drones from H226  onwards
Elan has been expanding into AI-related applications, including edge-AI applications
such as smart-city and ADAS for commercial vehicles, leveraging its edge AI and
computer vision capabilities to pivot beyond its traditional consumer HMI business. It has
also started shipments of ISP (Image Signal Processors) and local dimming IC for Chinese

drone customers from mid-2025, lifting overall AI-related revenue to 7% of total sales in
Q126, up from 4% in Q125. It is now working with Taiwanese drone makers and
transforming its business model from providing ISP , touch IC and local dimming
controllers, into full-stack camera modules (ASP ranging from US$300-400 for fixed
focus to US$500-600 for zoom configurations, rising to over US$1,000 for thermal
sensing cameras),  HMI controller modules for ground control stations (GCS), and AI

boxes, meaningfully expanding its content per drone as Elan is now positioned as a
system-level solutions provider, instead of a component/IC vendor.

According to Elan, its drone product offering now includes AI target detection and
tracking, AI visual navigation, obstacle avoidance, a flight control system,  BLDC
(brushless direct current) motor, and both swarm and long-distance communication
modules. The ground control station (GCS) complements this with similarly

differentiated hardware: a waterproof, glove-operable touchscreen, a mini-LED local
dimming display solution for better sunlight readability, precision joystick, fingerprint
reader, and smart antenna arrays, which are purpose-built for military-grade useability in
harsh outdoor environments. The GCS is priced at NT$70-90k per unit, while
management noted the local dimming controller module alone (priced at US$150–200
per GCS unit) faces limited competition given the dual requirement of operating

underwater and with gloved touch, providing a meaningful differentiating factor versus
peers.

Although the Taiwan government's annual budget (including drone spending) is still
under review, management highlighted that the opportunity for its drone business is
primarily for the overseas market. Elan noted the global UAV drone shipment forecast of

11.2mn units in 2024, with consumers accounting for 54%, enterprise 29%, and
military 17%. It expects the global enterprise and military-use drone shipments to reach
a combined 5.5mn units in 2026, much larger than the domestic volume estimated at
~30k per annum, based on the Taiwan government's plan of ~200k units over six years.
Notably, the overseas market is increasingly seeking non-China supply chain

alternatives, which Elan believes could be a structural tailwind for its positioning.
In the near-term, Elan will begin shipping higher value-added modules for its domestic

500 units per month. Management guides drone-related revenue to reach a low-single-
digit share of total revenue in 2026F, scaling to over 5%  in 2027F with some upside, and

potentially reaching 10% of total sales in 2028F.

Partnering with PETA to capture NPO/CPO opportunities
Elan announced at its analyst meeting that it has made a strategic investment in the US-

based silicon photonics startup PETA Optronics,  acquiring approximately a 15–16%
stake and becoming its largest institutional shareholder, while the founding
management team retains majority ownership.  Under this partnership, PETA is expected
to focus on the development and supply of photonic integrated circuits (PICs), while Elan
is responsible for developing electronic IC (EIC) components, such as  transimpedance
amplifiers (TIAs) and driver ICs, jointly delivering a comprehensive optical module

solution. Management highlighted that the partnership leverages Elan's optical sensing
know-how at subsidiary Eminent and Elan's SiGe (Silicon–Germanium) design expertise,
which are particularly relevant for TIA and driver ICs.
In terms of product roadmap, PETA is advancing quickly in high-speed optical
interconnects with 800G and 1.6T pluggable transceiver modules, with 1.6T pluggable
transceiver module shipments targeted in 2026 followed by a transition to in-house PIC

deployment by 2027. PETA's current PIC technology supports 200G per channel, with
ongoing development toward 400G per channel. The company’s customer base
primarily consists of US-based module houses in California and Texas, while its FAU
partner is a leading Taiwan optical module manufacturer. Tower Semi is its current
foundry partner but it plans to expand to additional  partners including TSMC for NPO
and CPO applications.
Beyond pluggable transceiver, PETA and Elan are jointly developing 3.2T NPO solutions

for data center scale-out demand, with engineering samples anticipated by early 2027
and commercial products by late 2027, targeting to supply into  US CSPs. Looking further
ahead, PETA is also progressing on CPO development, with expectations to begin
recognizing NRE revenue from CPO projects in 2026 and targeting 6.4T CPO product
readiness by 2028.
Given the uncertainties of technology development and the lengthy qualification
process for NPO/CPO EICs, we do not include the potential revenue contribution from

EIC into our estimates for Elan for now. Nevertheless, with the  ramp of higher value-
added modules for its drone customers from H226, its capability to offer solutions that
can operate underwater and  with gloved touch, and the overseas drone market seeking
for non-China supply chain partners, we expect robust growth for its drone business.
We think this could support Elan's overall AI-business to achieve a 49% sales CAGR in
2025-30E,  outpacing its traditional HMI business.

Elan Microelectronics 15 June 2026 ab 9
Elan Microelectronics UBS Research
What´s Priced In?
WHAT´S PRICED IN?Elan stock has traded sideways over the past month, although year-to-date its share
price is up 24%, underperforming the TAIEX's +53%. We believe the underperformance
is mainly because the market generally associates Elan with NB demand, given its
roughly 90% sales exposure to that segment. Nevertheless, we think investors could be
overlooking the potential ASP uplift benefits coming from HMI spec upgrades and new

opportunities in the edge AI business. Based on UBS estimates, Elan stock is trading at
15.4x/12.5x 2026E/2027E PE, or 14x 12-month forward consensus EPS, which is near
the middle of its historical 7-20x range and below current peer averages. The stock is
also supported by a ~6% dividend yield. We believe the improving earnings outlook on
content growth for the touch business and AI opportunities has not been fully priced in,
and we expect the stock to further re-rate as its revenue and earnings growth

outperforms NB shipments, especially as its foreign institutional investor (FINI) holdings
are below 24%, versus the 30-50% of previous upcycles.

Risk to the current share price skewed (1.8:1) to the upside
Elan is trading at NT$151.0  (as of  15 June 2026).
UPSIDE (NT$225): Our upside scenario reflects better-than-expected demand for NBs
and faster adoption of premium HMI features, and a quicker ramp of drone and edge-AI
applications. Our upside scenario lifts 2026/2027E sales growth from our 9%/16% base
case to 17%/17%, with GM expanding from 47.4%/48.1% to 48.1%/48.6% and OPM
rising from 23.3%/25.5% in our base case to 24.0%/26.0%. Our upside valuation of NT

$225 assumes 17.5x 2027E P/E.
BASE (NT$188): We expect 9%/16% YoY sales growth in 2026/2027, with a GM of
47.4%/48.1%, based on sustaining market position and content gain increases despite
overall weaker NB demand of -10% YoY units decline in 2026E. Our price target of NT
$188 is based on 16x 2027E P/E, the average upcycle P/E after 2020.
DOWNSIDE (NT$110): We assume  downside risk from softer NB demand and slower
adoption of HMI features as well as delay of AI-related opportunities (drones, ADAS). In

our downside scenario, we assume 2026/2027 sales growth of -3%/+6% YoY, with
2026/2027E GM falling to 46.9%/47.6% and  OPM contracting to 21.8%/24.0%. Our
downside valuation of NT$110 assumes 12x 2027E P/E.

Forecast returns

Forecast excess return 23.6%
Company Description
Elan Microelectronics is a Taiwan-based fabless IC design house specialising in human-
machine interface including touchscreen controllers, touchpad modules, pointing stick
modules and fingerprint sensors, specifically for notebook PCs. It started with

microcontrollers (MCUs) for PC peripherals and consumer electronics upon establishment in
1994, and further expanded the reach into notebook PC input devices through the
acquisition of a related subsidiary under KOA Corporation in 2003.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on a target PE multiple.
Fabless integrated circuit (IC) design houses face a variety of uncertainties, including rapidly
changing technology, end-market demand, competition, employee turnover and foundry

wafer costs. They are also vulnerable to legal disputes over intellectual property rights.
For Elan, we believe key risks include: 1) PC and NB demand dynamics, with any slowdown in
these end-markets potentially directly impacting Elan's growth and its new product roll-outs;
2) a longer-than-expected product qualification cycle or slower-than-expected product
adoption among clients, particularly for new products; 3) fiercer-than-expected price
competition from global and Asian peers negatively impacting growth and gross margin; and

5) faster-than-expected foundry and assembly cost increases negatively impacting gross
margin.
kevinlu@ lenovo.comkevinlu@ lenovo.com

Quantitative Research Review
UBS Global Research publishes a quantitative assessment of its analysts' responses to certain questions about the likelihood of an occurrence
of a number of short term factors in a product known as the 'Quantitative Research Review'.  The views for this month can be found below.
Views contained in this assessment on a particular stock reflect only the views on those short term factors which are a different timeframe to

the 12-month timeframe reflected in any equity rating set out in this note.  For previous responses please make reference to (i) previous UBS
Global Research reports; and (ii) where no applicable research report was published that month, the Quantitative Research Review which can
be found at
https://neo.ubs.com/quantitative, or contact your UBS sales representative for access to the report or the Quantitative Research
Team on ubs-quant-answers@ubs.com. A consolidated report which contains all responses is also available and again you should contact your

UBS sales representative for details and pricing or the Quantitative Research Team on the email above.
Elan Microelectronics
Question Response

getting better, N/A = no view)

deteriorate over the next six months? Rate on a scale of 1-5 (1 = getting tougher

3 = no change, 5 = getting better, N/A = no view)
3

change/getting worse for this stock? Rate on a scale of 1-5 (1 = getting a lot
worse, 3 = not much change, 5 = getting a lot better, N/A = no view)

4. Relative to the current CONSENSUS EPS forecast, is the next company EPS
update likely to lead to:  (1 = negative surprise vs consensus, 3 = in-line with
consensus, 5 = positive surprise vs consensus expectations, N/A = no view)

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

### Page 1

Taiwan Small Cap

What's new: We went on a marketing trip to Hong Kong and Singapore, 
alongside the Daiwa Tech Conference (Acter, Sinbon, Fositek and Insyde) 
from 9-12 June. Overall, sentiment remained positive, likely on improving AI 
server demand and intact order visibility from GPU and ASIC projects.

What's the impact: Taiwan thermal solutions. We believe now is a good 
time to accumulate thermal solution names, aiming for a stronger 2H26 
revenue outlook vs. 1H26. Despite 2Q26 revenue being relatively subdued 
for most of the thermal solution names we cover, we expect the revenue 
outlook to improve in 2H26, as projects such as VR and ASICs begin to 
ramp up in 2H26. We believe structural and favourable ASP upgrade trends 
are set to support fundamentals over the medium to long term. We also 
expect multiple applications to adopt thermal solutions such as 
infrastructure, training, inference, CX-9 and space thermal management. 
Our preferred thermal solutions names: Jentech, AVC and Auras.

Taiwan cable solutions. We believe cable suppliers should benefit from 
the AI (data cables and power cables) and non-AI segments such as rising 
industrial applications under AI infrastructure. Our preferred cable solutions 
names: Bizlink and Sinbon.

Update on the names we invited to the Daiwa Tech Conference in 
Hong Kong and Singapore. Acter’s (5536 TT, not rated) management 
remains positive on its order backlog (TWD55bn as of 1Q26) and expects 
to continue improving its project mix, thereby expanding gross margin 
(25.4% in 1Q26, see flash, 24 March); Sinbon’s management maintained 
its 2026 revenue guidance of TWD36-36.5bn, with expectations of YoY 
revenue growth of 15% for medical, +40% for auto, +8-10% for green 
energy, +15% for industrial and flat to -5% YoY for the communications 
segment (see flash, 18 May). Fositek maintains its revenue guidance of 
flat to 5% YoY growth for 2Q26, despite its QTD revenue accounting for 
60% of Daiwa’s and Bloomberg consensus 2Q26 revenue estimates, and 
up by double digit % QoQ for 3Q26 revenue, in light of the ramp-up of 
VR200 and Trainium 3 projects (see flash, 1 June). Insyde is positive on 
the OpenBMC firmware market, along with the AI acceleration trend and 
rising security applications (see flash, 4 May).

What we recommend: Overall, our top picks for 2H26 are Jentech, AVC, 
Auras, Bizlink, Fositek, Sinbon, Innodisk, Eclat and Nien Made. Downside 
risks: weaker-than-expected end-market demand.

How we differ: We are one of the few brokers to provide marketing 
feedback and the 2H26 outlook for Taiwan Small- and Mid-Caps.

Wistron’s May revenue was flat MoM, or 13% above our estimate, which we attribute
to the company’s strong rack-level AI server and general servers solid demand. We
model sequential revenue declines in Jun-Aug, considering PC consumption pull-in in
1H26 and rack-level AI server model transition. Despite the MoM decline, our

estimate still assumes double-digit YoY growth for Jun-Aug revenues, reflec ting our
positive view on Wistron serving global leading brand makers, better riding on the
growing AI infrastructure trend. The company’s global capacity expansion in Vietnam
and US (link) should also support their AI servers business in coming years. Maintain
Buy.

2026E-28E net income forecasts by 20% / 17% / 20%, mainly on higher revenues.

business performance, and (2) rising GPU and ASIC AI server shipments growth,
along with new models entering in 2H26. Our 2026E opex ratio is slightly revised

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

TUC (627 4.TWO)
Sales/GM upside potential; Buy
CITI'S TAKE
CCL price hike is ongoing with TUC and EMC continuously posting monthly
sales uptick in April and May. We expect the price hike to continue in June
and 2H26E. EMC just posted unaudited May ESP likely implied to GM
upside on price hike and enhanced product mix. We believe TUC would also
benefit from the CCL tightness given its more aggressive pricing strategies.

Thus, we raise our TP to NT$1,950 (25x 2027E EPS) to factor in strong price
hike benefit and open a 30-day positive Catalyst Watch on upcoming
monthly sales/unaudited earnings upside potential. Buy.
Enhanced product mix and aggressive price hike — Our industry check suggests
that leading CCL makers like EMC may be facing capacity constraint and cherry-
picking of orders, resulting in enhanced product mix or aggressive price hike to those
non-AI demand. We expect TUC either to receive some outflowing orders or to hike the

prices to customers continuously. We believe TUC’s pricing would be more aggressive
on those non-AI demand and be at least on par with EMC for AI customers.
Read-across from EMC’s unaudited May earnings beat on price hike — EMC just
reported unaudited May EPS of NT$9.07 vs 1Q26 Citi/BBG forecasts of NT$19.5/21.7.
We think EMC unaudited May EPS likely implies GM profile of up to range 34-35%
in May, higher than EMC’s previous 2Q26 guidance range of 29.5-32.5%. T o note

that, EMC would see pricing benefit as well in June. We believe TUC would also see
the price hike benefits as EMC does in 2Q26, leading to a GM beat in 2Q26.
CCL supply tightness continued with potential price hike again in 2H26 — We
think CCL industry supply side will just increase 20-30%/30-40% YoY in 2026/27E,
which we believe couldn’t catch up with PCB capacity expansion pace or end-
customer demand. Even if CCL makers now are converting their capacity from mid-

to-low end capacity to high-end one, we think it wouldn’t alleviate the tightness in
the near term. We expect another round of CCL price hike in 2H26 given the high
season impact. TUC’s new Thailand capacity now targets to ramp by end-3Q26,
which would help the company to secure more demand going forward.
Earnings/TP raise. Buy — We lift our 2026/27 /28E earnings by 14%/25%/24% to
factor in better benefits from price hike. We lift our TP to NT$1,950 (25x 2027E EPS)

Buy
Catalyst Watch: Upside

### Page 7

TUC (6274.TWO)
18 June 2026 Citi Research
7
TUC
Company description
TUC was established in 1974 and mainly produced optical glass initially. Since
1997, it started to provide copper clad laminate (CCL) and prepreg.
Headquartered in Hsinchu, Taiwan, TUC has an extensive global service
network spanning mainland China, Japan, Korea, the US and Germany. It has
been specializing in high-speed CCL for networking and now has a strong
position in supplying 800G switch opportunities. The company also started

to supply AI ASIC from 2025.
Investment strategy
We expect increasing AI ASIC exposure for TUC as it continues to see new
project opportunities among US CSP customers, including the high-end CCL
market for Trainium chip. We like TUC as it has production expertise in high-
speed CCL products, especially in networking, such as 800G, and has long
built solid relationships with upstream high-end materials suppliers to ensure
raw material support. We rate TUC a Buy given meaningful 800G ramp from

2026E onwards and its increasing opportunities in AI projects within CSPs
and enterprise players.
Valuation
Our target price for TUC is set at NT$1,950, based on a target PE multiple of
25x on our 2027E EPS. We believe our target PE multiple, at the peak of TUC’s
PE average in the last upcycle, is justified by its strong margin expansion
potential on a higher sales mix from AI ASIC server/800G products and price
hikes on supply tightness. We believe TUC’s growth outlook is well supported

by AI ASIC and 800G with its capacity ramp in Thailand from 2Q26 onward.
At our target price, the shares would trade at 2026E/27E PB of 19.4x/10.8x.
Risks
Citi’s quant system rates TUC High Risk given high share price volatility, which
we largely attribute to frequent debates over the spec upgrade for next-gen AI
servers. In general, we believe some investors initially tend to have high hope
for aggressive spec upgrade without sufficiently considering the scalability

and cost structure of CCL products, which then leads to a shortfall in the end.
However, from a longer-term perspective, we see a decent and clear spec
upgrade trend with meaningful ASP growth potential among AI servers of
different generations. As such, we do not assign a High Risk rating.
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) a slower-than-expected CCL upgrade trend; 2) weaker-than-
expected AI server/800G demand; 3) production bottlenecks in AI supply

chain (e.g., glass, copper foil, foundry, or OSAT); 4) unexpected share loss in
key AI server projects; 5) flexible practice of OOC policy by end customers;
and 6) slow development of the PCB industry in Southeast Asia.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.
